Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on segmentation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 752. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 580 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to segmentation theory, we decompose the problem into 752 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 580. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
